# Regulatory claims audit

This document records a review of public copy in the Zelta and FinAegis projects for statements that could be read as
regulated services, licences, partnerships, card availability, compliance outcomes, offered tokens, yield, insurance or
investment offers, and the change made to each.

> FinAegis is open-source software. Using it does not make a deployment compliant. Licensing and regulatory
> compliance are the responsibility of the operator of each deployment. Nothing here is legal advice.

> Zelta is non-custodial wallet software. Zelta and its operator are not a bank, electronic money institution,
> payment institution or crypto-asset service provider, and do not hold, exchange or transmit users' funds.
> Any card or off-ramp services will be provided by licensed third parties under their own terms.

## Scope

Reviewed surfaces: the zelta.app website, the finaegis.org website (both served from this repository), this repository's
README, documentation and package listings, the Zelta mobile app's user-visible strings and store listing text, and the
finaegis.github.io index.

Risk levels: **high** — could be read as an unlicensed regulated activity, use of card-network marks, an offered
asset-referenced token, or a yield/insurance promise; **medium** — misleading or implies a partnership, endorsement or
compliance outcome; **low** — tone or minor wording.

Principles applied: no new claims were added; wording was removed or softened rather than rewritten; card services are
described only as planned; third-party providers are described as integration adapters with no partnership or
endorsement implied; GCU is described only as a software demonstration.

| Surface | Changes | High-risk |
|---|---|---|
| zelta.app (Zelta website, production) — landing, legal pages, investor/CGO pages, SEO metadata | 113 | 44 |
| GCU (Global Currency Unit) — demo pages, JSON-LD, API descriptions, GCU docs | 214 | 52 |
| finaegis.org — main pages (home, about, partners, compliance, security, sub-products, support, navigation) | 218 | 76 |
| finaegis.org — feature and developer pages, user-facing PHP strings | 217 | 51 |
| GitHub — README, docs index, feature docs, partner docs, package and SDK listings | 209 | 28 |
| GitHub — vision docs, user guides, CGO docs | 97 | 18 |
| GitHub — architecture, API, developer, operations and archive docs | 162 | 35 |
| Follow-up consistency fixes | 37 | 0 |
| Fixes from the review pass | 133 | 15 |
| finaegis.github.io (FinAegis open-source index) | 8 | 0 |
| **Total** | **1408** | **319** |

The Zelta mobile app and its store listing text were reviewed in the same pass (239 changes); that audit table is kept in the app's own repository.


## Withdrawn documents

These documents were replaced with a short "withdrawn" notice because their main content could not be fixed with line edits:

- `docs/01-VISION/FINANCIAL_INSTITUTION_REQUIREMENTS.md` — partner-institution and deposit-protection content
- `docs/01-VISION/REGULATORY_STRATEGY.md` — licensing-strategy content
- `docs/01-VISION/SUB_PRODUCTS_OVERVIEW.md` — licensing, returns and deposit-protection content
- `docs/01-VISION/WEBSITE_CONTENT_STRATEGY.md` — marketing plan built on deposit-protection and partner claims
- `docs/05-USER-GUIDES/CGO-USER-GUIDE.md` — token-sale and investment content
- `docs/05-USER-GUIDES/LIQUIDITY_POOLS_GUIDE.md` — yield and rewards content
- `docs/05-USER-GUIDES/P2P_LENDING_GUIDE.md` — investment-returns and regulated-platform content
- `docs/05-USER-GUIDES/STABLECOIN_GUIDE.md` — token redemption, yield and insurance content
- `docs/archive/GCU_USER_VISION.md` — GCU offered-product, deposit-protection and yield content

## GCU (Global Currency Unit)

GCU exists in this codebase only as a software demonstration: database records in the demo environment, with no smart
contract, no on-chain deployment and no public distribution. All GCU copy now carries this disclaimer:

> The Global Currency Unit (GCU) is a software demonstration — a reference implementation built with FinAegis.
> It is not issued, offered or sold to anyone and has no monetary value. Any GCU balances, conversions or
> basket "votes" shown in the demo are simulated. Offering to the public in the EU a token that references a
> basket of currencies and/or commodities would require authorisation as an asset-referenced token issuer under
> MiCA (Title III); no such authorisation is held.

## Items not changed in this pass (pending owner decision)

These need a product or legal decision, a binary asset, or a change outside the repository, so they were not edited here:

- Route availability: some demo routes (GCU voting/trading, CGO concept pages, investor page, exchange, liquidity, GCU API)
  are registered outside the promotional-pages gate and are therefore reachable on zelta.app. Copy on them was corrected;
  whether to restrict them to the demo environment is pending.
- Operator legal entity details (name, legal form, company code, registered address) in the footer and Terms are pending
  confirmation. No details were invented; `TODO(regulatory)` comments mark the places.
- Social preview images (`public/images/og-default.png`, `public/images/og-twitter.png`) contain card artwork and need to
  be regenerated.
- `docs/archive/litas_specification.pdf` (concept document) cannot be edited in place.
- GitHub repository description, npm/MCP-registry listings and app-store listings are published outside this repository
  and need to be updated separately once these changes are approved.
- An older, private Zelta landing-page prototype (separate repository, not deployed) still contains card-network copy.
